Together they recast the exception as a linked safe-conduct network that members construct for one another.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"103:1","root":"ع ص ر","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001019","branch_id":"B005","role":"Refuge, rescue, and taking protection trigger a spatial-social reading of the focus exception."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ء م ن","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000054","branch_id":"B001","role":"Settled security and trust supply the protected condition produced inside the network."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"و ص ي","source_word_indices":["6","8"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001656","branch_id":"B001","role":"Joining makes refuge an interlinked relation rather than an isolated shelter."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ح ق ق","source_word_indices":["7"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000347","branch_id":"B007","role":"A due that must be defended identifies what the refuge network protects."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ص ب ر","source_word_indices":["9"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000840","branch_id":"B003","role":"Standing surety with someone gives each member responsibility for another's continued safety and endurance."}],"structural_cues":["All four focus verbs are plural, and تواصوا is reciprocal.","The exception begins with assent/security and ends with interpersonal surety-bearing branches of patience."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies refuge, security, joining, protected dues, and surety; I infer that the plural practices construct a social refuge. A live alternative is a private refuge image with no institutional or interpersonal architecture.","changed_reading":{"before":"Each believer escapes loss by possessing the listed virtues.","after":"People become an exception by making a linked refuge of trust, guarded claims, and mutual liability for one another."}},{"model_id":"delta_humanization_by_mutual_presence","status":"revised","confidence":"strong","trigger_roots":["ء ن س"],"mechanism":"The generic human of 103:2 is activated as a being opposed to wild estrangement and capable of familiarity. The focus then reads as a movement from isolated generic humanity into plural, trust-bearing, reconciliatory mutual presence; sociality is part of the rescue mechanism, not incidental packaging.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"103:2","root":"ء ن س","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000059","branch_id":"B001","role":"The human as manifest and non-wild supplies the contrast between humanization and estranged or feral isolation."},{"source_ref":"103:2","root":"ء ن س","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000059","branch_id":"B003","role":"Familiar presence that removes estrangement supplies the relational condition toward which the focus moves."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ء م ن","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000054","branch_id":"B001","role":"Security and trust make mutual presence inhabitable rather than threatening."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ص ل ح","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000876","branch_id":"B002","role":"Reconciliation removes social aversion and gives sound action a specifically relational outcome."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"و ص ي","source_word_indices":["6","8"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001656","branch_id":"B003","role":"Mutual exhortation performs the repeated person-to-person contact through which the plural group is sustained."}],"structural_cues":["103:2 uses the generic singular الإنسان; 103:3 answers with الذين and four plural verbs.","The reciprocal form تواصوا occurs twice, making relation grammatically conspicuous."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies generic humanity, non-wild manifestation, familiarity, reconciliation, trust, and reciprocal address; I infer a transition from isolated humanity to enacted human fellowship. The live alternative is that الإنسان is only a species label and contributes no social contrast.","changed_reading":{"before":"Some members of humanity individually add faith, deeds, truth, and patience.","after":"The generic human is reconstituted as a trustworthy plural through reconciliation and repeated mutual address; becoming-with-others is itself part of the exception."}},{"model_id":"delta_collective_perception_of_truth","status":"new","confidence":"medium","trigger_roots":["ء ن س"],"mechanism":"The perception branches of ءنس shift الحق from a proposition merely possessed to reality kept perceptible. Through mutual exhortation, members detect, voice, and mirror what is real for one another, so truth survives blind spots through distributed witnessing.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"103:2","root":"ء ن س","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000059","branch_id":"B002","role":"Detecting by sight, sensation, or hearing supplies the perceptual act by which truth becomes noticed."},{"source_ref":"103:2","root":"ء ن س","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000059","branch_id":"B005","role":"The human image in the pupil supplies a contained mirror-image for one person making another visible."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ح ق ق","source_word_indices":["7"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000347","branch_id":"B001","role":"Correspondence to established reality is the object that distributed perception tracks."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ح ق ق","source_word_indices":["7"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000347","branch_id":"B005","role":"Making truth stand turns detection into public confirmation rather than private impression."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"و ص ي","source_word_indices":["6","8"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001656","branch_id":"B003","role":"Reciprocal exhortation distributes noticing and confirmation across multiple observers."}],"structural_cues":["The human noun in 103:2 is followed in the focus by plural agents who repeatedly address one another.","الحق is governed by the first of two reciprocal exhortation clauses."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies sensory detection, the person-image in the pupil, reality-correspondence, confirmation, and mutual address; I infer a distributed witnessing system. The live alternative is that the ocular image is lexical residue with no mechanism in this construction.","changed_reading":{"before":"Mutual exhortation communicates a truth already fully seen.","after":"Mutual exhortation is also how finite people help one another perceive, test, and keep reality publicly visible."}},{"model_id":"delta_loss_ledger","status":"revised","confidence":"strong","trigger_roots":["خ س ر"],"mechanism":"Commercial loss activates the transaction branch of عمل and the owned-claim branch of حق. The focus becomes a counter-economy: trust enables dealings, sound action repairs corrupt exchange, concrete rights remain on the ledger, and reciprocal correction prevents advantage from being purchased through another's deficit.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"103:2","root":"خ س ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000409","branch_id":"B002","role":"Commercial loss supplies a ledger in which action, entitlement, and deficit can be related."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ء م ن","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000054","branch_id":"B001","role":"Trust and trustworthiness supply the credit condition on which dealings depend."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ع م ل","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001046","branch_id":"B005","role":"Dealing or transacting between people makes عمل socially and economically relational."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ص ل ح","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000876","branch_id":"B001","role":"Righting against corruption supplies repair of damaged or exploitative dealings."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ح ق ق","source_word_indices":["7"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000347","branch_id":"B003","role":"A person's owned right places concrete entitlements on the moral ledger."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"و ص ي","source_word_indices":["6","8"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001656","branch_id":"B003","role":"Mutual correction provides ongoing peer oversight of the counter-economy's dealings."}],"structural_cues":["The focus exception immediately follows the statement that the human is in خسر.","Plural action is followed by two reciprocal clauses, allowing private accounting to become shared oversight."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies trade loss, trust, interpersonal dealing, repair, owned rights, and mutual charge; I infer that these form one ledger-like economy. The live alternative is generic spiritual loss with no commercial structure.","changed_reading":{"before":"Faith and good deeds are assets that offset an individual's abstract loss.","after":"The exception establishes trustworthy, reparative dealings in which another's due is not converted into one's own gain and peers keep the ledger answerable."}},{"model_id":"delta_measure_calibration","status":"new","confidence":"medium","trigger_roots":["خ س ر"],"mechanism":"Giving short measure makes loss a distortion introduced at the act of valuation. Mutual exhortation to الحق becomes peer calibration of how claims, actions, and persons are measured; الصبر restrains both opportunistic under-measuring and impulsive overcorrection.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"103:2","root":"خ س ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000409","branch_id":"B003","role":"Short measure or balance supplies the concrete failure of calibration that produces another's deficit."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ح ق ق","source_word_indices":["7"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000347","branch_id":"B001","role":"Correspondence to what is established supplies the standard against which measure is checked."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ح ق ق","source_word_indices":["7"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000347","branch_id":"B003","role":"Concrete entitlement identifies who is harmed when valuation is shortened."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ص ل ح","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000876","branch_id":"B001","role":"Repair against corruption gives calibration a corrective aim."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"و ص ي","source_word_indices":["6","8"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001656","branch_id":"B003","role":"Reciprocal exhortation makes calibration mutual, including correction of the corrector."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ص ب ر","source_word_indices":["9"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000840","branch_id":"B001","role":"Self-restraint prevents fear, anger, or appetite from biasing measure and response."}],"structural_cues":["The indefinite خسر in 103:2 is immediately answered by a plural exception with reciprocal correction.","الحق and الصبر occupy parallel complements of repeated تواصوا."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies short measure, established truth, owned rights, repair, mutual charge, and restraint; I infer that exhortation functions as calibration. A live alternative is that short measure remains only one illustration of generic loss and does not organize the focus.","changed_reading":{"before":"People tell one another the truth and separately tell one another to endure.","after":"People repeatedly recalibrate one another's measures of reality and entitlement, while reciprocal restraint keeps correction itself from becoming another false measure."}},{"model_id":"delta_anti_diminution_container","status":"new","confidence":"exploratory","trigger_roots":["ع ص ر","خ س ر"],"mechanism":"Successive time plus general diminution activates a conservation model: suitable work is joined into a tightly made form, truth holds its fit, and patience acts as boundary and seal. The focus practices do not merely accumulate value; they reduce leakage and disintegration.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"103:1","root":"ع ص ر","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001019","branch_id":"B001","role":"Successive time supplies the duration over which an assembled good can erode or be retained."},{"source_ref":"103:2","root":"خ س ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000409","branch_id":"B001","role":"General diminution supplies the leakage or attrition that the focus mechanism counters."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ص ل ح","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000876","branch_id":"B003","role":"Suitability makes each action fit the point at which loss is occurring."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"و ص ي","source_word_indices":["6","8"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001656","branch_id":"B001","role":"Joining supplies continuity between persons, acts, truth, and restraint."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ح ق ق","source_word_indices":["7"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000347","branch_id":"B010","role":"Tight-made firmness supplies resistance to unraveling under attrition."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ص ب ر","source_word_indices":["9"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000840","branch_id":"B018","role":"The stopper image gives patient restraint the role of sealing what would otherwise escape."}],"structural_cues":["The context sequence moves from time in 103:1 to diminution in 103:2 and then to the exception in 103:3.","The focus chain alternates action and reciprocal linkage before ending on الصبر."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies duration, diminution, fit, joining, tight construction, and a stopper; I infer a leakage-and-retention mechanism. A live alternative is that these material branches are independent images and should not be assembled into one container model.","changed_reading":{"before":"The exception adds enough positive merits to outweigh loss.","after":"The exception is also an anti-attrition structure: fitted action, reciprocal joining, firm truth, and patient sealing prevent value and relation from leaking away through time."}}],"surprising_valid_outliers":[{"outlier_id":"outlier_traveling_cohort","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This is surprising because it combines technical road, horse-step, and rocky-ground branches rather than likely senses of the surface forms. It remains anchored in عملوا، تواصوا، الحق، and الصبر and is sharpened by successive العصر; render it only as a material analogy for coordinated persistence, never as a lexical translation.","focus_anchor":"The plural progression from عملوا through two تواصوا clauses to الصبر can carry a coordinated-travel mechanism.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"103:1","root":"ع ص ر","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001019","branch_id":"B001","role":"Successive time extends the group's movement into a journey rather than a single act."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ع م ل","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001046","branch_id":"B011","role":"The traveled road supplies a path made durable by repeated passage."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"و ص ي","source_word_indices":["6","8"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001656","branch_id":"B001","role":"Joining keeps the travelers connected along the path."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ح ق ق","source_word_indices":["7"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000347","branch_id":"B014","role":"The matched hoofstep supplies coordination in which later steps land where guiding steps have held."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ص ب ر","source_word_indices":["9"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000840","branch_id":"B005","role":"Hard stone and gravelly ground supply the resistance across which coordinated persistence matters."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Good action and patience are properties carried by separate people.","after":"Exploratorily, the plural is a traveling cohort: repeated practice wears a road, mutual linkage coordinates steps, and patient solidarity crosses resistant ground."}},{"outlier_id":"outlier_cultivated_pasture","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"The pasture sense of وصي and cloud sense of صبر are form-distant, so this cannot gloss تواصوا or الصبر. The reading remains packet-anchored because suitability, repeated reciprocal connection, and the contextual rain branch form a coherent ecological mechanism; downstream prose should label it a cultivation analogy.","focus_anchor":"The pairing of الصالحات with repeated تواصوا and final الصبر permits an exploratory reading of collectively maintained conditions for flourishing.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"103:1","root":"ع ص ر","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001019","branch_id":"B003","role":"Rain-bearing cloud supplies the environmental input that makes a cultivated field possible."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ص ل ح","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000876","branch_id":"B003","role":"Suitability supplies the fit between an action and the living need it serves."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"و ص ي","source_word_indices":["6","8"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001656","branch_id":"B004","role":"Pasture favorable to grazing animals supplies the image of conditions that nourish rather than merely command."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"و ص ي","source_word_indices":["6","8"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001656","branch_id":"B003","role":"Mutual exhortation assigns cultivation to reciprocal communal activity."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ص ب ر","source_word_indices":["9"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000840","branch_id":"B010","role":"Layered cloud contributes sustained accumulation rather than a single transient shower."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"The group transmits imperatives to truth and patience.","after":"As a contained ecological analogy, the group cultivates a suitable shared pasture in which truthful connection and accumulated endurance let lives flourish."}},{"outlier_id":"outlier_bitter_dose_through_constriction","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This reading is deliberately odd: it combines a small drink for a choking obstruction, bitter medicine, and a penetrating thrust. Each image remains tied to العصر, الصبر, and الحق, and repeated تواصوا supplies an administering relation; render only as an experiential analogy for difficult corrective speech, not as morphology or doctrine.","focus_anchor":"The final sequence تواصوا بالحق وتواصوا بالصبر can sustain an exploratory mechanism in which difficult truth is made passable through a measured dose of endurance.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"103:1","root":"ع ص ر","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001019","branch_id":"B008","role":"A small drink that lets a choking obstruction pass supplies the measured enabling dose."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ص ب ر","source_word_indices":["9"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000840","branch_id":"B008","role":"The bitter medicinal extract makes patience remedial precisely because it is difficult to take."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ح ق ق","source_word_indices":["7"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000347","branch_id":"B009","role":"The straight penetrating thrust gives truth the risky capacity to reach beneath a defended surface."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"و ص ي","source_word_indices":["6","8"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001656","branch_id":"B003","role":"Mutual exhortation makes the difficult dose something people administer and receive reciprocally."},{"source_ref":"103:3","root":"ص ل ح","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000876","branch_id":"B001","role":"Repair against corruption constrains penetration toward healing rather than injury."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Truth is stated and patience means tolerating the result.","after":"Exploratorily, reciprocal patience is the bitter measured medium that lets penetrating truth pass through constriction and become reparative rather than wounding."}}],"summary":{"strongest_changes":["The focus changed from a four-item virtue list into a reciprocal maintenance circuit whose exception must be continuously produced.","الحق expanded from abstract truth to established reality, due obligation, concrete entitlement, guarded claim, and a standard of measure.","الصبر expanded from private endurance to restraint, surety with another, boundary, and sealing against attrition.","العصر changed the circuit into both a temporal relay and, through its pressure branches, a stress-test that extracts enacted proof.","الإنسان and خسر activated social humanization, distributed perception, commercial accountability, and mutual calibration as coexisting mechanisms."],"what_was_special":"The repeated تواصوا proved to be the hinge: it could join intervals, transfer a charge, distribute perception, guard rights, calibrate measure, build refuge, and contain loss without collapsing those readings into one. Material branches added live architectures of workshop, joint, ledger, road, field, and sealed container while remaining explicitly analogical.","remaining_uncertainties":["The pressure and refuge branches of العصر are mechanically powerful but remain alternatives to the straightforward successive-time activation.","Commercial loss and short measure may specify the general diminution of خسر or may be illustrative branches that should remain separate.","The perceptual reading of ءنس is coherent with reciprocal truth-telling, but the human noun may activate only generic humanity rather than sensing or mirroring.","The fit, container, travel, cultivation, and medicinal models are anchored but branch-distant; their value is abductive and analogical, not lexical equivalence.","الحق supports truth, obligation, and owned right simultaneously; the focus does not force a single one of these social scopes."]}}}},"v12_reader_walks":{"reader_a":{"activated_readings_md":"1. **An anti-loss economy.**  \n   Lexical evidence: `ء م ن` B001/B002, secure trust and trusting assent; `ع م ل` B001/B004/B005, intentional work, wage, and transaction; `ص ل ح` B001/B003, soundness and suitability; `و ص ي` B003, mutual exhortation; `ح ق ق` B002/B003, due obligation and owned right; `ص ب ر` B001, self-restraint. Activated by `خ س ر` B002/B003 in 103:2 and `ع ص ر` B002/B007 in 103:1. Structural cue: `إلا` makes the whole chain an exception to loss, and the sequence moves from inner trust to enacted work to reciprocal maintenance. Assigned role: faith secures credit/trust; deeds put value into practice; sound acts are fit assets; mutual truth protects the standard; mutual patience prevents panic-selling or abandonment under pressure. The packet supplies the commercial, pressure, trust, work, right, and restraint branches. I infer the economy in which these elements counteract loss. Reading change: the verse reads as a complete anti-deficit mechanism, not a loose list of virtues.\n\n2. **A repaired human network.**  \n   Lexical evidence: `ء م ن` B001, security/trust; `ص ل ح` B002, reconciliation; `و ص ي` B001/B003, joining and mutual charge; `ح ق ق` B001/B005, firm truth and establishing it; `ص ب ر` B003, suretyship or standing with someone. Activated by `ء ن س` B003/B006 in 103:2, companionship and intimate associate. Structural cue: all verbs in the exception are plural, and `تواصوا` is reciprocal and repeated. Assigned role: belief supplies trust, reconciliation removes social estrangement, mutual charge joins persons across time, truth gives shared reality, and patience makes members bear one another’s burden. The packet supplies the social branches. I infer that the plural exception repairs the social creature named in the previous ayah. Reading change: 103:3 becomes a social architecture: humans are rescued from loss by becoming a trustworthy, truth-bearing, patient network.\n\n3. **A pressure apparatus with fitted parts.**  \n   Lexical evidence: `ع ص ر` B002 from 103:1, pressing; `ع م ل` B002, putting something to work; `ص ل ح` B003, fitness; `ح ق ق` B010/B011, tight-made firmness and fitted socket; `ص ب ر` B004/B018, sides/brim and stopper. Structural cue: the exception follows the press-oath and the \"inside loss\" phrase, then names elements that can function like structure, fit, sides, and seal. Assigned role: works are the operated parts; righteous/sound acts are parts fit for the task; truth is the tight joint or socket; patience is the side or stopper that prevents leakage under pressure. The packet supplies the material images; I infer the apparatus relation from their sequence after `العصر`. Reading change: the verse becomes almost mechanical: under the compression of time, only a fitted structure of trust, action, truth, and patience holds.\n\n4. **A legal and testamentary community.**  \n   Lexical evidence: `و ص ي` B002/B003, charge, testament, mutual exhortation; `ح ق ق` B002/B003/B004, obligation, owned right, contested claims; `ص ب ر` B002/B012, compelled oath/execution and legal requital; `ء م ن` B001, safe-conduct/trust; `ع م ل` B003/B005, official charge and dealings. Activated by `خ س ر` B002/B003, commercial loss and short measure. Structural cue: the verse repeats `تواصوا بـ`, creating two transmitted obligations, first to right/truth and then to endurance. Assigned role: the believers are entrusted parties; deeds are administered obligations; truth is the due claim; patience is the capacity to remain under binding process; mutual counsel is a living testament passed among them. The packet supplies legal, transactional, and testamentary branches. I infer that the exception describes a community that prevents loss by maintaining rights and obligations. Reading change: the verse is not only devotional; it can read as a covenantal/legal order that resists fraud, contested claims, and collapse.\n\n5. **A season of cultivation and endurance.**  \n   Lexical evidence: `ع ص ر` B003/B010, rain-clouds and grain enclosed in husks; `ص ل ح` B003, suitability; `و ص ي` B004, pasture suiting grazing animals; `ح ق ق` B008/B013, due bearing age and fulfilled animal condition; `ص ب ر` B010/B011, layered cloud and piled food. Structural cue: later branches cluster around growth, pasture, maturity, cloud, and stored food, while 103:1 already allowed rain-bearing `العصر`. Assigned role: faith is settled trust in the season; work is cultivation; sound deeds are fit growth; truth is ripeness or due maturity; patience is cloud layering, food accumulation, and waiting through the season. The packet supplies the ecological branches. I infer that the four practices name conditions by which life moves from threatened loss into yield. Reading change: the exception can be heard as a cultivated ecology: rescue is not instant subtraction from loss but patient maturation under a fitting season.\n\n6. **Bitter medicine and disciplined swallowing.**  \n   Lexical evidence: `ع ص ر` B008, small sips to pass a choking morsel, and `ص ب ر` B008, bitter aloe-like drug; supported by `و ص ي` B003, repeated mutual exhortation, and `ء م ن` B003, \"amen\" as request for response. Structural cue: the last item is patience, and the repeated counsel creates dosage-like recurrence. Assigned role: truth may be hard to swallow; patience is bitter medicine; mutual counsel administers it in repeated, survivable measures; trust and prayerful assent keep the patient receptive. The packet supplies the choking, bitter extract, repetition, and response-request branches. I infer the therapeutic arrow: counsel helps the constricted human metabolize truth. Reading change: the verse becomes pastoral and medicinal, not only judicial: the exception survives loss by repeatedly taking what is bitter but healing.","retrospective_surprises_md":"No later ayah follows 103:3, so no later-ayah surprise emerged. The main retrospective pressure came from looking back to 103:1 and 103:2: the exception is far less like a detached virtue list after the full pass, because the press of `العصر` and the interior deficit of `خسر` make each term function as part of a rescue mechanism.","turkish_prose_synthesis_md":"İstisna, dağınık erdemler listesi gibi değil, kayba karşı kurulmuş bir düzen gibi okunuyor. İman güven ve tasdik zemini sağlar; amel değeri işe koşar; salih ameller uygun ve sağlam işi temsil eder; hak, ölçüyü ve sabit borcu korur; sabır ise baskı altında dağılmayı önleyen tutma gücüdür. `تواصوا`nun iki kez gelişi bunu sosyal bir mimariye dönüştürür: insanlar birbirine hakikati ve dayanmayı taşır, birbirinin yükünün yanında durur. Başka bir etkinleşen okumada bu yapı mekaniktir: zamanın presi altında hak bir yuva/eklem, sabır ise kenar veya tıpa gibi sızıntıyı önler. Hukuki okumada ise ayet, hakların, emanetlerin ve bağlayıcı öğütlerin korunduğu bir topluluk düzenidir. Tarımsal okumada kurtuluş, uygun mevsimde sabırla olgunlaşan verimdir; tıbbi okumada ise hak bazen yutması zor, sabır acı ama şifalı bir ilaçtır.","source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-tr/s103/full_context_control/reader_s103_a_ayah_walk.md"},"reader_b":{"activated_readings_md":"1. The exception as a complete rescue-system rather than a list of virtues.\n   Lexical evidence: fixed `ء م ن B001` (settled security/trust), `ء م ن B002` (trusting assent), `ع م ل B001` (intentional doing), `ص ل ح B001` (soundness/rightness against corruption), `و ص ي B003` (mutual exhortation), `ح ق ق B001` (firm truth against falsehood), and `ص ب ر B001` (self-restraint from panic). Activators: `خ س ر B001` (general deficiency) and `ع ص ر B001` (recurring time). Structural cue: the ayah is introduced by `إِلَّا`, then proceeds through belief, work, sound acts, two reciprocal exhortations, truth, and patience. Assigned role: trust stabilizes the heart; work externalizes it; soundness repairs corruption; mutual exhortation keeps truth and restraint circulating under time-pressure. Abductive move: I supply the systems arrow, that each item protects the next from collapsing back into loss. Reading change: the exception is not a private moral inventory but a mutually reinforcing mechanism for surviving the attrition named in 103:1–2.\n\n2. A reparative economy that reverses trade-loss and short measure.\n   Lexical evidence: fixed `ع م ل B004` (wage/pay for work), `ع م ل B005` (dealing/transaction), `ح ق ق B002` (due obligation), `ح ق ق B003` (owned right), `و ص ي B002` (a charge joined to another person), and `و ص ي B003` (mutual exhortation), activated by `خ س ر B002` (trade loss) and `خ س ر B003` (shorting measure/weight). Structural cue: the loss statement precedes a sequence of work, rights, and reciprocal charges. Assigned role: righteous work pays into the deficit; truth/right restores due measure; mutual exhortation prevents the right from remaining unclaimed or forgotten. Abductive move: I infer that the exception reverses loss by restoring correct accounting between persons. Reading change: `آمَنُوا وَعَمِلُوا الصالحات` becomes not only piety but a counter-ledger: faith-backed labor and truth-counsel repair the economy of deficit.\n\n3. A craft and repair reading: sound structure made under pressure.\n   Lexical evidence: fixed `ع م ل B002` (putting something to work/using it), `ع م ل B006` (manual labor crews), `ص ل ح B001` (making right/repair against corruption), `و ص ي B001` (joining one thing to another), `ح ق ق B010` (tight-made firmness in fabric or speech), `ح ق ق B011` (fitted socket/exact middle), and `ص ب ر B004` (top/side/brim). Activators: `ع ص ر B002` (pressing) and `خ س ر B001` (diminution). Structural cue: verbs of doing and mutual joining surround nouns of soundness, truth, and patient holding. Assigned role: the community is a made object: work shapes it, rightness repairs it, mutual counsel joins it, truth fits it into place, patience forms its edges and load-bearing sides. Abductive move: I supply the material analogy that ethical sequence can be read as construction under stress. Reading change: the ayah reads like instructions for building a durable human fabric or vessel that does not leak away under `العصر`.\n\n4. Mutual covenant and succession.\n   Lexical evidence: fixed `ء م ن B001` (trust/entrustment), `و ص ي B001` (joining), `و ص ي B002` (charge, testament, instruction), `و ص ي B003` (mutual exhortation), and `ص ب ر B003` (surety/standing with someone), activated by basmalah `ر ح م B002` (kinship bond). Structural cue: `تَوَاصَوْا` appears twice, making reciprocity the most audible repeated action of the ayah. Assigned role: faith supplies trustworthiness; testamentary charge carries responsibility from one person to another; patience is surety that the charge will be borne. Abductive move: I infer that repeated mutual exhortation makes the saved group a chain of entrusted persons rather than a set of isolated believers. Reading change: the exception becomes covenantal continuity: each person is rescued by receiving and transmitting truth and endurance.\n\n5. Patience as hard, bitter, and pressured endurance, not passive calm.\n   Lexical evidence: fixed `ص ب ر B006` (severe inescapable trouble), `ص ب ر B007` (harsh winter cold), `ص ب ر B008` (bitter aloe-like drug), `ص ب ر B005` (hard stone/gravelly ground), and `ص ب ر B001` (self-restraint from panic). Activators: `ع ص ر B004` (whirlwind/rising dust), `ع ص ر B002` (pressing), `ح ق ق B012` (exhausting hard pace), and `ع م ل B007` (self-exertion/taking trouble). Structural cue: patience comes last, after truth, as the second object of repeated mutual exhortation. Assigned role: patience names the necessary hardness and bitter medicine that lets truth survive pressure, wind, cold, and rough ground. Abductive move: I supply the experiential arrow, that the final term describes the conditions under which the previous terms must be maintained. Reading change: `الصبر` is not quiet resignation; it is a rough, medicinal, load-bearing endurance that must itself be mutually taught.","retrospective_surprises_md":"No later ayah follows 103:3, so there was no later surprise in packet order. The retrospective change came from rereading 103:3 through the earlier ayat: `العصر` made the whole exception answer pressure and attrition, while `لَفِي خُسْرٍ` made the exception feel like a change of enclosure. The basmalah’s `ر ح م B002/B003` also made the social mechanics of `تَوَاصَوْا` feel unexpectedly kin-like and womb-like: truth and patience are not only advised; they are carried, guarded, and re-given within a mercy-shaped bond.","turkish_prose_synthesis_md":null,"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-tr/s103/full_context_control/reader_s103_b_ayah_walk.md"}},"v12_reader_walks_wide":{"reader_a":{"activated_readings_md":"1. **A causal practice circuit rather than a flat list.** **Lexical evidence:** ء م ن `B002` (trusting assent), ع م ل `B002` (putting something to work), ص ل ح `B001` (soundness against corruption), و ص ي `B003` (mutual exhortation), ح ق ق `B001` (firm truth), and ص ب ر `B001` (self-restraint from panic). **Structural cue and role:** assent is followed by operative work, then by two formally parallel reciprocal acts; trust supplies orientation, work makes it effective, soundness is the output criterion, truth supplies correction, and restraint keeps correction active under strain. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies order and parallelism, while I infer the enabling arrows and the feedback loop among the stages. **Reading change:** the exception reads as a self-maintaining practice system in which no listed element remains safely private or inert.\n\n2. **The exception constructs a refuge through mutual guarantee.** **Lexical evidence:** ء م ن `B001` (security and trust), و ص ي `B001` (joining), و ص ي `B003` (mutual charging), and ص ب ر `B003` (surety or standing with someone), activated by ع ص ر `B005` (refuge). **Structural cue and role:** `إلا` exits the enclosure `في خسر`, but every actor in the exception is plural and the final operations are reciprocal; security names the refuge-state, joining gives it structure, and patient surety makes members answerable for one another. **Abductive move:** I infer that dependable relations produce the refuge rather than merely describing people who independently reached safety. **Reading change:** `آمنوا` carries a social security-and-trust dimension, while `الصبر` becomes solidarity and borne liability as well as private endurance.\n\n3. **The sequence repairs short measure by restoring what is due.** **Lexical evidence:** ع م ل `B005` (dealing between people), ص ل ح `B002` (reconciliation), ص ل ح `B003` (fitness to a situation), ح ق ق `B002` (due obligation), and ح ق ق `B003` (a particular owned right), activated by خ س ر `B003` (shorting measure). **Structural cue and role:** sound acts precede reciprocal counsel `بالحق`; fitted action supplies practical restitution, reconciliation repairs the relation, and `الحق` supplies the due standard and claimant's right. **Abductive move:** I infer that the exception reverses loss by ending under-delivery and restoring full measure in human dealings; the packet does not identify particular claims. **Reading change:** `الصالحات` and `الحق` become context-fitted acts of repair and due-giving, not merely generic goodness followed by abstract correctness.\n\n4. **Mutual truth-speaking disciplines rival claims.** **Lexical evidence:** ح ق ق `B004` (contesting claims to right), `B005` (making truth stand), ص ل ح `B002` (reconciliation), و ص ي `B003` (mutual exhortation), and ص ب ر `B001` (restraint). **Structural cue and role:** the truth phrase is governed by a reciprocal verb rather than unilateral declaration, and it is followed by an identically formed patience phrase; competing claims supply the social pressure, mutual exchange exposes them, verification establishes what stands, reconciliation preserves relation, and restraint blocks panic or aggression. **Abductive move:** I infer an adjudicative process from the branch images and syntax; mutuality alone does not guarantee agreement. **Reading change:** `تواصوا بالحق` can describe a disciplined communal method for bringing truth through contest, with `تواصوا بالصبر` making that method sustainable.\n\n5. **A charge relayed across recurring time.** **Lexical evidence:** و ص ي `B001` (joining one thing to another), `B002` (a charge or testament joined to another person), and `B003` (mutual exhortation), activated by ع ص ر `B001` (recurring time). **Structural cue and role:** `تواصوا` is repeated, and each occurrence transfers a durable content, first truth and then restraint; joining supplies continuity, the testament image supplies transmissibility beyond one speaker, and recurrence supplies the temporal field. **Abductive move:** I infer that reciprocal charging can preserve and relay these contents across persons and intervals, not just within one conversation. **Reading change:** the two `تواصوا` clauses read as a continuity institution that resists the losses of passing time.\n\n6. **Parallel counsel weaves a durable social structure.** **Lexical evidence:** ح ق ق `B010` (firm, tightly made speech or weaving), و ص ي `B001` (joining), and ص ب ر `B003` (standing with a group). **Structural cue and role:** the matched clauses `وتواصوا بالحق / وتواصوا بالصبر` join repeated verbal strands with distinct contents; firm speech supplies tensile quality, joining supplies linkage, and standing together bears the resulting load. **Abductive move:** this is a material analogy inferred from grammatical parallelism and the accepted branch images, not a fabric explicitly named by the ayah. **Reading change:** the closing is audible as construction: reciprocal, well-set speech binds separate humans into a structure capable of surviving pressure.","retrospective_surprises_md":"No later ayah exists in the packet, so no later-ayah surprise emerged. On retrospective rereading, the earlier pressure/refuge pair in 103:1 and the trade-loss/short-measure pair in 103:2 continued to sharpen the exception's productive, fiduciary, and reparative roles, but they did not supply a later activation.","turkish_prose_synthesis_md":null,"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-tr-11ayah/s103/full_context_control/reader_s103_a_ayah_walk.md"}},"v12_cross_run_publication":{"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-cross-run/tr/103_ayah_findings_publication.json","protocol":"v12-cross-run-publication-v3","language":"tr","surah":103,"ayah_ref":"103:3","canonical_ayah_ref":"103:3","baseline":{"source_ref":"103:3","text":"Ancak inanan ve iyi işler yapan, birbirlerine gerçeği tavsiye eden ve birbirlerine sabrı tavsiye edenler hariç.","target_tokens":[["Ancak",["103:3:1"]],["inanan",["103:3:2","103:3:3"]],["ve",["103:3:4"]],["iyi",["103:3:5"]],["işler",["103:3:5"]],["yapan",["103:3:4","103:3:5"]],["birbirlerine",["103:3:6"]],["gerçeği",["103:3:7"]],["tavsiye",["103:3:6","103:3:7"]],["eden",["103:3:6"]],["ve",["103:3:8"]],["birbirlerine",["103:3:8"]],["sabrı",["103:3:9"]],["tavsiye",["103:3:8","103:3:9"]],["edenler",["103:3:2","103:3:8"]],["hariç",["103:3:1"]]]},"findings":[{"text":"İman, eylem, onarım, hak ve sabır düz bir erdem listesi değil; Önceki ayetler bu diziyi şaşırtıcı biçimde bir erdem listesi olmaktan çıkarır: güven kalbi sabitler, iş ve salihlik bozuk yapıyı onarır, hak eksik ölçüyü düzeltir, karşılıklı vasiyet bu hakkı dolaşımda tutar, sabır ise baskı, acılık ve zor şartlar altında bu yapıyı taşıyan dayanıklı kenar hâline gelir. {ء م ن B001; ء م ن B002; ع م ل B001; ع م ل B004; ص ل ح B001; و ص ي B001; و ص ي B002; و ص ي B003; ح ق ق B001; ح ق ق B002; ح ق ق B010; ص ب ر B001; ص ب ر B005; ص ب ر B008}","arabic_text":"إِلَّا ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَعَمِلُوا۟ ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ وَتَوَاصَوْا۟ بِٱلْحَقِّ وَتَوَاصَوْا۟ بِٱلصَّبْرِ","reading_text_tr":"İstisna edilenler iman eden, salih işler yapan, hakkı ve sabrı birbirine tavsiye eden topluluktur. A charge remains effective because someone is designated to carry it, a guarantor stands behind it, and the right itself imposes a duty of protection.","ayah_refs":["103:3"]},{"parent_channel":"Entrusted Rights and Enforcement","parent_semantic_invariant":"A binding claim is carried through appointed roles, contested speech, reconciliation, custody, or requital.","parent_surprising_reach":"Reciprocal exhortation expands into testamentary succession, surety, compelled testimony, and authoritative requital.","key":"B","name":"Contested Right Reconciled by Deliberation","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Opposing claimants each assert a right; reciprocal communication and adjudication convert the contest into restored concord.","active_motifs":"adversarial rights dispute `ح ق ق:B004/m01`; adjudication and verification `ح ق ق:B005/m02`; peacemaking `ص ل ح:B002/m01`; restored social concord `ص ل ح:B002/m02`; reciprocal communication `و ص ي:B003/m02`; human collective `ء ن س:B001/m01`","ayah_anchors":"103:2 `إِنسَٰنَ`; 103:3 `ٱلْحَقِّ`, `ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ`, and both occurrences of `تَوَاصَوْا۟`","synthesis":"The dispute is not erased but processed. Competing claims are stated and tested, communication remains reciprocal, and reconciliation removes the estrangement generated by the contest.","ayah_refs":["103:2","103:3"]},{"parent_channel":"Entrusted Rights and Enforcement","parent_semantic_invariant":"A binding claim is carried through appointed roles, contested speech, reconciliation, custody, or requital.","parent_surprising_reach":"Reciprocal exhortation expands into testamentary succession, surety, compelled testimony, and authoritative requital.","key":"C","name":"Coercive Custody and Compelled Oath","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A person or living target is externally held, prevented from leaving, and in one legal extension forced to swear.","active_motifs":"restraint and withholding `ع ص ر:B006/m01`; captive held for execution `ص ب ر:B002/m01`; living target held for shooting `ص ب ر:B002/m02`; oath compelled by authority `ص ب ر:B002/m03`; human person `ء ن س:B001/m01`","ayah_anchors":"103:1 `عَصْرِ`; 103:2 `إِنسَٰنَ`; 103:3 `ٱلصَّبْرِ`","synthesis":"The voluntary holding of patience is reversed into externally imposed holding. Custody fixes a body in place, while the compelled oath fixes speech under institutional pressure.","ayah_refs":["103:1","103:2","103:3"]},{"parent_channel":"Entrusted Rights and Enforcement","parent_semantic_invariant":"A binding claim is carried through appointed roles, contested speech, reconciliation, custody, or requital.","parent_surprising_reach":"Reciprocal exhortation expands into testamentary succession, surety, compelled testimony, and authoritative requital.","key":"D","name":"Requital Satisfying a Claim","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An established entitlement is answered by exact retaliation or an execution carried out under authority.","active_motifs":"exacting retaliation `ص ب ر:B012/m01`; execution by requital `ص ب ر:B012/m02`; established entitlement `ح ق ق:B002/m02`; incurred loss `خ س ر:B001/m01`","ayah_anchors":"103:2 `خُسْرٍ`; 103:3 `ٱلصَّبْرِ`, `ٱلْحَقِّ`","synthesis":"Loss becomes a claim that demands a proportionate answer. The lexical scene moves from deprivation to a formally satisfied right, with authority converting the claim into requital.","ayah_refs":["103:2","103:3"]},{"parent_channel":"Work, Exchange, and Accounted Return","parent_semantic_invariant":"Labor or property is evaluated by the return it yields, the right it creates, and the measure by which gain or shortfall is known.","parent_surprising_reach":"Failed return acquires the tactile hardness of gravel and the bitterness of medicinal sap, while quantitative loss becomes an underfilled measure.","key":"A","name":"Labor Crew and Earned Provision","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Manual workers perform material tasks and receive a wage or extracted yield whose absence would constitute loss.","active_motifs":"manual laboring crew `ع م ل:B006/m01`; digging and building labor `ع م ل:B006/m02`; wage or provision `ع م ل:B004/m01`; extracted yield or revenue `ع ص ر:B007/m02`; entitlement to return `ح ق ق:B002/m02`; general loss `خ س ر:B001/m01`","ayah_anchors":"103:1 `عَصْرِ`; 103:2 `خُسْرٍ`; 103:3 `عَمِلُوا۟`, `ٱلْحَقِّ`","synthesis":"Work is organized as a crew-based productive process. Effort produces a claim to wages and yield, so diminution is legible as the failure of labor to receive its due return.","ayah_refs":["103:1","103:2","103:3"]},{"parent_channel":"Work, Exchange, and Accounted Return","parent_semantic_invariant":"Labor or property is evaluated by the return it yields, the right it creates, and the measure by which gain or shortfall is known.","parent_surprising_reach":"Failed return acquires the tactile hardness of gravel and the bitterness of medicinal sap, while quantitative loss becomes an underfilled measure.","key":"B","name":"Transaction, Capital, and the Hard Return","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A dealing places capital and proprietary claims at risk, ending either in retained value or an unprofitable return.","active_motifs":"interpersonal transaction `ع م ل:B005/m01`; capital loss or absence of profit `خ س ر:B002/m01`; proprietary claim `ح ق ق:B003/m01`; gift entering circulation `ع ص ر:B007/m01`; coarse hard stone `ص ب ر:B005/m01`; bitter medicinal extract `ص ب ر:B008/m01`","ayah_anchors":"103:1 `عَصْرِ`; 103:2 `خُسْرٍ`; 103:3 `عَمِلُوا۟`, `ٱلْحَقِّ`, `ٱلصَّبْرِ`","synthesis":"The commercial frame joins dealing, ownership, transfer, and the possibility of losing principal. Hard stone and bitter extract provide sensory analogues for a bargain whose return is resistant and unpleasant.","ayah_refs":["103:1","103:2","103:3"]},{"parent_channel":"Work, Exchange, and Accounted Return","parent_semantic_invariant":"Labor or property is evaluated by the return it yields, the right it creates, and the measure by which gain or shortfall is known.","parent_surprising_reach":"Failed return acquires the tactile hardness of gravel and the bitterness of medicinal sap, while quantitative loss becomes an underfilled measure.","key":"C","name":"Pressed Yield and Short Measure","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Material is put through a press, collected as extract, then assessed by measure; an underfilled scale converts production into loss.","active_motifs":"pressure-based extraction `ع ص ر:B002/m01`; juice or expressed yield `ع ص ر:B002/m03`; putting a tool to use `ع م ل:B002/m02`; deficient measure or weight `خ س ر:B003/m01`; unmeasured bulk-food sale `ص ب ر:B011/m03`","ayah_anchors":"103:1 `عَصْرِ`; 103:2 `خُسْرٍ`; 103:3 `عَمِلُوا۟`, `ٱلصَّبْرِ`","synthesis":"Pressing transforms raw material into a countable return. The heap sold without weighing and the deliberately reduced measure expose the point at which productive extraction becomes quantitative deprivation.","ayah_refs":["103:1","103:2","103:3"]},{"parent_channel":"Maturation, Fitness, and Provision","parent_semantic_invariant":"A living being or food source becomes usable when it reaches maturity, receives fitting conditions, and is protected or accumulated.","parent_surprising_reach":"One readiness threshold spans adolescent growth, a camel's carrying age, a work animal's stamina, pasture suitability, crop enclosure, and stored food.","key":"A","name":"Maturity into Working Capacity","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A young person or animal crosses a developmental threshold and becomes physically ready for adult or load-bearing activity.","active_motifs":"girl reaching youth or puberty `ع ص ر:B009/m01`; camel reaching carrying and riding age `ح ق ق:B008/m01`; breeding maturity `ح ق ق:B008/m02`; capable working camel `ع م ل:B008/m02`; strengthened animal condition `ح ق ق:B014/m02`; attained soundness `ص ل ح:B001/m01`","ayah_anchors":"103:1 `عَصْرِ`; 103:3 `ٱلْحَقِّ`, `عَمِلُوا۟`, `ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ`","synthesis":"Maturity is defined functionally rather than merely chronologically. ## Your response

Write in tr. Write exactly four files at the paths below and modify nothing else. Do not return one combined response. Omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the artifact name.

- Prose: `_commentary/outputs/s103/103_3.prose.tr.md`
- Evidence surface: `_commentary/outputs/s103/103_3.evidence.tr.md`
- Findings index: `_commentary/outputs/s103/103_3.index.tr.md`
- Friction: `_commentary/outputs/s103/103_3.friction.tr.md`

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. **The findings index** — one line per reading carried by the prose, under its bundle ref, with `[inference]` on the writer's own readings. Include each required `surprise:<id>` synthesis row and its `[supports-primary]` or `[shifts-primary]` relation.
4. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing — and end it with the density audit required by the task document. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision. Add no bulk without a distinct
reader payoff, but do not compress a significant finding for brevity.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. They do not license recurrence, maturity, or a surah-wide
  channel name.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by related root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, developed through its final reader-visible result
   in proportion to its significance.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

Start each paragraph with reader meaning before grammar. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi
Allah'a verir; bunu fiille değil, sabit bir ad cümlesiyle yapar" over "Bu âyet,
tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik bir hüküm kurar."

Keep the prose anchored in this ayah's own wording without imposing a paragraph
or length quota. Give every significant finding enough space to make its
mechanism and reader payoff clear, even when it arrives through later context;
never compress it merely to keep the commentary short. Preserve every member of
the resonance set, and use a plain closing synthesis only when the ayah benefits
from one.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
confirm in friction that there was no live misconception, replacement risk, or
counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
